# Draft: full main-to-dev sync preserving dev DSv4 and Muon

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/pull/7960) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Purpose and frozen inputs

Draft for integration review and CI. Keep draft until current-head validation and the independent-review follow-up are complete; do not merge. This is the full pinned main-to-dev sync, not another 100-commit slice. The three merged slices #7906, #8112 and #8209 (300 distinct upstream commits) and their repair commits are already in the ancestry.

- dev: `7b150acfacee9d1126025f6cbe0f49c588975c51`
- main: `850bf4db59961c08923fe9dc9dae3044dce44b07` (not refreshed for this follow-up)
- Current signed source head: `05f0721bc241fce56935de7e5b9f8e5099bd3cc3`
- Current source tree: `2dcf3deac8eaf4e316862a37c512e4e134ff34cc`

This supersedes the earlier provisional #7336/#7469 draft and its main-first DSv4 disposition.

## Current status (05f0721b)

This head adds a test-local, restoring FLA L2-normalization tuning configuration for the two SBHD/THD layout-comparison methods. Production defaults, compilation, BF16, every original assertion/tolerance, and test limits remain unchanged.

The earlier cache-reset-only attempt did **not** fix the failure. A GB200 stage diagnostic reproduced the exact 19/245760-element mismatch on ranks 1–3 and localized the earliest difference to two normalized K values; projection and convolution were identical. With this fixture fix, **all four GB200 ranks pass 14 tests, with the same 4 existing SP skips**, and every captured stage in the original failing padding case has zero numeric differences on all ranks. Evidence: [GPU job 496437955](https://gitlab-master.nvidia.com/dl/jet/ci/-/jobs/496437955), versus [red comparison 496362492](https://gitlab-master.nvidia.com/dl/jet/ci/-/jobs/496362492). This is targeted validation, not full-PR acceptance; diagnostic CI files are not in this PR.

The preceding DSv4 fixture fix (`25b85c15`) supplies packing-scheduler, per-rank capacity and contiguous-layout metadata without changing its inputs or numerical assertions. Its CPU metadata checks and lint pass; its dedicated CP CUDA-graph GitHub job passed, while packed-CP numerical coverage is still being checked in the full GB200 scope.

- Fresh current-source GitHub CI: [39285738131, attempt 1](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/actions/runs/39285738131).
- Fresh GitLab dev-only acceptance: [75046054](https://gitlab-master.nvidia.com/ADLR/megatron-lm/-/pipelines/75046054), overlay `4ccd2c18` on source `05f0721b`.
- These new runs have been launched, **not declared green**. The previous 61/61 dev/H100 unit-bucket pass ([75004895](https://gitlab-master.nvidia.com/ADLR/megatron-lm/-/pipelines/75004895)) belongs to source `9c1adb75`.
- Personal OCI-HSG allocation remains queued for whole-file, uninstrumented GDN and packed-DSv4 qualification.
- Waiting for the three requested independent reviews (astra, opus, kimi), then reconciling their findings against the latest source before readiness.

## Resolution decisions

- **DSv4:** retain dev's dynamic CP, balanced indexer, compact BF16/MXFP8, fused compressor and THD paths. Keep main's static ModuleSpecs, per-layer configuration and independent PP/mHC interfaces. Preserve inverse-RoPE ownership and runtime layout, precision, dependency and applicable unsupported-combination guards.
- **Muon:** retain explicit split-QKV/per-head handling across GTP reconstruction and padded rank-shard restoration, alongside main's nonsplit/layer-sharded paths. Compact LayerWise synchronization uses its own buffer policy, including mixed Muon/AdamW configurations. LayerShardedMuon remains unsplit-only.
- **Integration:** reconcile mHC/MTP/recompute/hash-routing contracts and main's logger/RL APIs. Keep full caller MTP masks separate from decoder-local masks; use the runtime CP group and correct SP layout. Preserve dev's native mHC aggregation ownership/backward behavior. Map main's packaged MLA backward tuning keys to dev's retained out-of-place interface without claiming retuning.
- **Tests:** retain main's partitioning/selective-test policy with dev additions. Preserve all 325 chunk-schedule cases across six bounded buckets, all 100 early-wgrad cases, and all 57 GDN output-norm cases across local/distributed files. Retain all 54 unique dispatcher capacity cases while removing 36 duplicate executions. Original 30-minute limits and numerical assertions remain unchanged.
- **Dependencies:** the retained integrated environment follows pinned main's NGC 26.09 / TE 2.20.2 / Python >=3.12, with reconciled dev-specific dependencies. It is not identical to dev's 26.04 / TE 2.14 baseline. This follow-up does not change dependency pins.
- **Governance:** dev CODEOWNERS is unchanged. No quarantine/exemption labels, numerical tolerance changes or timeout increases.

## Previous repair attempt (9c1adb75)

This follow-up changes four test files, without production-kernel, dependency, golden, tolerance or timeout changes:

- Replace the stale GPT mHC/PP/MTP rejection test with real MTP construction on the correct PP stage; additionally compare actual PP1/PP2 losses and gradients in eager and recompute modes.
- Match production gradient finalization in the FP8/GTP harness: fence GTP after every backward, including when DP gradient overlap is off. Otherwise pending output-layer wgrads survive model teardown and can later use destroyed process groups. The new pending-gradient assertions fail on the old helper and pass after the real flush; no gradients are discarded as a workaround.
- Isolate the GDN compiler cache per test case. The failed GB job had already exhausted Dynamo's default recompile budget at Parameter-versus-Tensor CP views before its SBHD/THD mismatch. Keep the default budget, compilation, BF16 and all original numerical assertions; share the compiler cache within each comparison.

Current validation: the complete mHC pipeline file passes 92 tests per H100 rank (8 ranks), and dense/MoE LayerShardedMuon passes 2 tests per rank (4 ranks) with the new no-pending-wgrad assertions. Exact-tree autoformat exits 0 (non-gating mypy diagnostics are not a type-check pass); strict kernel/golden gates pass for 1227 changed files and 31 golden files.

The isolated OCI-HSG ARM64/GB200 environment uses the pinned CI image from pipeline 74967540. The personal allocation remains queued; the separate GitLab GB200 diagnostic above has now verified the targeted normalization fix. Full current-head acceptance is still pending. No new full 61-bucket result is claimed.

## October 19 CI follow-up

Based on GitHub run [39205578252, attempt 1](https://github.com/NVIDIA/Megatron-LM/actions/runs/39205578252) at prior source `9fc37722ca5c2671c28e6a7b1c56381c478d56d3`:

1. Remove the parent-only `split_qkv_per_head=False` argument from LayerShardedMuon construction; explicitly reject true instead of silently dropping an unsupported mode.
2. Fix the merged FP8 helper overwriting its explicit `num_steps` with 100. Restore both parents' intended requested 3/6/8/30 steps and default 100; assert returned trajectory lengths. No tolerance or output slicing changes.
3. Update the fused pre-GDR test and packed reference helper to dev's actual headwise-CP APIs, keeping all CP-local parameter/head-count and numerical assertions. Audit all 23 pre-GDR calls in the SSM tests for stale keyword names.
4. Give the GDN parallel-test module a restoring synchronous-Inductor compilation context, matching existing GDN2/output-norm fixtures. Compilation and numerical checks remain enabled.
5. Restore #8112's DDP-only source for LayerWise parameter layout; the builder no longer injects a conflicting optimizer default. Cover both DDP layout values.
6. Restore dev's supported GPT mHC+PP+MTP path: main's constructor rejection was inappropriate with the retained decoder/MTP multistream implementation. Add a real BF16 two-stage model forward/backward regression that checks the contracted/multistream boundary and nonzero finite MTP/input gradients.
7. Recover five dropped upstream golden-file updates, **byte-for-byte from pinned main**, with provenance in #6991/#7217. All 450 loss and 450 num-zeros values from the failed jobs' full-precision artifacts exactly match those existing upstream values. The unchanged repository comparator passes all five comparisons. These files were not generated from this CI run; the other 922 existing dev golden blobs remain unchanged.

## Earlier validation

Previous follow-up at `0b6f0d51b1aa` (not a new-head full-suite claim):

- `BASE_REF=dev CHECK_ONLY=true bash tools/autoformat.sh`: exit 0 on the exact current tree. The non-gating mypy diagnostics are **not** a type-check pass.
- Strict kernel-coverage gate: 1227 changed files. Strict changed-golden gate: 31 files. Both pass without exemptions.
- CPU checks verify requested FP8 iteration binding, normal/exceptional compiler-setting restoration, exact upstream golden blobs and original functional-result comparisons.
- CW targeted GPU regressions: GPTModel whole file **23 passed/rank on 8 ranks**, including real mHC+PP+MTP forward/backward; training plumbing **15 passed/rank on 8 ranks**; fused/packed GDN, CP-local parameters, sequence-metadata validation and the emerging-optimizer file **154 passed/rank on 8 ranks, 3 original CP=1 skips**; LayerShardedMuon dense/MoE end-to-end **2 passed/rank on 4 ranks**, with the requested eight-step trajectories. For previous head `0b6f0d51b1aa`, the GDN/Muon runs used source bytes identical to that commit; the earlier GPT/plumbing source differs only in the subsequent GDN helper correction and golden-file EOF formatting. These are targeted regressions, not a fresh full 61-bucket run.
- The LayerShardedMuon client disconnected, but the original Slurm step completed with exit `0:0` and all four rank logs confirm both passes. The transport-failure record is retained; an accidentally duplicated launch was cancelled and is not counted. Local launchers now check live Slurm steps before retrying.

Historical baseline, **not a new-head full-suite claim**:

- Prior `9fc37722ca5c` / tree `add8ca8bcb52eebf54a11c2b37665a11df81b567` completed 61/61 canonical dev/H100 CW unit buckets under original marker filters and per-bucket limits. One pre-worker port collision was retried once on the identical snapshot; no cross-snapshot results were combined.
- Its GitLab dev-only pipeline [74935824](https://gitlab-master.nvidia.com/ADLR/megatron-lm/-/pipelines/74935824), child 74936515, also completed successfully with 61/61 unit buckets.
- Prior CW coverage included DSv4 packed CP graphs/replays, Muon/GTP, mHC/MTP/recompute, all six chunk-schedule buckets, and kernel replay tests. Existing skips/xfails/xpasses remain and are not evidence that unsupported backends or nondeterminism gaps are resolved.

## Remaining qualification

Fresh GitLab dev-only and GitHub default-scope CI are required for the current head. H100 checks do not qualify Blackwell-only MXFP8 numerics; the repaired iteration-count contract must still be revalidated there. The mHC unit regression is not a new full functional-training run.

CW/GitLab run dev unit tests, not LTS or functional recipes. GitHub retains its existing automatic scope without added labels. The two GitLab-only overlay files are excluded from the GitHub source branch. Unsupported HybridModel PP+EP-overlap combinations remain rejected. Optional DeepEP v2 / NVSHMEM bindings absent from the CW image are not claimed as covered.
